46 [Heading1] ABSTRACT
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "0", "after": "0", "line": "920", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "keepNext": true, "keepLines": true, "outlineLvl": {"val": "0"}, "pStyle": {"val": "Heading1"}, "pageBreakBefore": true, "jc": {"val": "center"}, "rPr": true}
 R {"{\"b\": true, \"bCs\": true, \"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"40\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"40\"}}": 1}
47 [Normal] The rapid growth of the film and media industry has increased the need for efficient project management and collaboration tools. Traditional methods of managing film production activities often involve manual paperwork, spreadsheets, and fragmented communication systems, which can lead to delays, mismanagement, and reduced productivity. To address these challenges, a web-based Film Production Management System named “Realcinephile Pictures: A Web-Based Film Production Management System” has been developed.
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "363", "after": "0", "line": "413", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "jc": {"val": "both"}, "rPr": true}
 R {"{\"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"24\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"24\"}}": 2, "{\"b\": true, \"bCs\": true, \"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"24\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"24\"}}": 1}
48 [Normal] The primary objective of this project is to provide a centralized platform for managing various aspects of film production, including project planning, script management, crew coordination, task assignment, production scheduling, budget tracking, asset management, and client communication. The system enables production teams to monitor project progress efficiently while ensuring better collaboration among stakeholders.
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "278", "after": "0", "line": "413", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "jc": {"val": "both"}, "rPr": true}
 R {"{\"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"24\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"24\"}}": 1}
49 [Normal] The application is developed using “HTML, CSS, and JavaScript” for the frontend, ‘PHP’ for backend processing, and ‘MySQL’ for database management. The system incorporates secure user authentication, role-based access control, reporting features, and an intuitive dashboard to simplify production workflows.
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "274", "after": "0", "line": "413", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "jc": {"val": "both"}, "rPr": true}
 R {"{\"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"24\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"24\"}}": 1}
50 [Normal] By digitizing and automating production management processes, the proposed system reduces administrative workload, improves resource utilization, enhances communication, and increases overall operational efficiency. The platform serves as a practical and cost-effective solution for film studios, production houses, and independent filmmakers seeking a modern approach to production management.
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "274", "after": "0", "line": "413", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "jc": {"val": "both"}, "rPr": true}

180 [Heading3] 1.1 Project Title
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "132", "after": "0", "line": "644", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "keepNext": true, "keepLines": true, "outlineLvl": {"val": "2"}, "pStyle": {"val": "Heading3"}, "rPr": true}

181 [Normal] Realcinephile Pictures: A Web-Based Film Production Management System
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "45", "after": "0", "line": "552", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "rPr": true}
 R {"{\"b\": true, \"bCs\": true, \"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"24\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"24\"}}": 1}
182 [Heading3] 1.2 Introduction to the System
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "0", "after": "0", "line": "644", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "keepNext": true, "keepLines": true, "outlineLvl": {"val": "2"}, "pStyle": {"val": "Heading3"}, "rPr": true}
 R {"{\"b\": true, \"bCs\": true, \"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"28\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"28\"}}": 1}
183 [Normal] Realcinephile Pictures is a web-based application designed to simplify and organize film production activities. The system provides a centralized platform for managing productions, scripts, crew members, schedules, tasks, budgets, assets, and reports. It helps production teams improve collaboration and monitor project progress efficiently.
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "43", "after": "0", "line": "552", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "rPr": true}
 R {"{\"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"24\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"24\"}}": 1}
184 [Heading3] 1.3 Project Overview
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "0", "after": "0", "line": "644", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "keepNext": true, "keepLines": true, "outlineLvl": {"val": "2"}, "pStyle": {"val": "Heading3"}, "rPr": true}
 R {"{\"b\": true, \"bCs\": true, \"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"28\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"28\"}}": 1}
185 [Normal] Film production involves multiple stages and departments that require proper coordination. Traditional management methods often lead to communication gaps and inefficient tracking. The proposed system digitizes these processes and provides an integrated environment for managing all production-related information through a single platform.

669 [Heading3] 16.1 Local Hosting using XAMPP
 P {"widowControl": true, "spacing": {"before": "135", "after": "0", "line": "644", "lineRule": "atLeast"}, "keepNext": true, "keepLines": true, "outlineLvl": {"val": "2"}, "pStyle": {"val": "Heading3"}, "rPr": true}
 R {"{\"b\": true, \"bCs\": true, \"color\": {\"val\": \"000000\"}, \"rFonts\": {\"ascii\": \"Times New Roman\", \"cs\": \"Times New Roman\", \"eastAsia\": \"Times New Roman\", \"hAnsi\": \"Times New Roman\"}, \"sz\": {\"val\": \"28\"}, \"szCs\": {\"val\": \"28\"}}": 1}
670 [Normal] The Realcinephile Pictures: A Web-Based Film Production Management System is hosted locally using the XAMPP server environment. XAMPP provides Apache and MySQL services required to run PHP-based web applications. It allows developers to test and deploy the system in a local environment before moving to a live server.
